Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The sr operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 11. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 831 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 11 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the sr structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 831. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the sr structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 959. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 246 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the sr decomposition. By minimality, the element a^959 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 246-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
